## `ARM-0129-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ARM` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Content Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Natural Latex Dry Rubber Content & DRC
- **Intro:** Content Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Natural Latex Dry Rubber Content & DRC — Pre-Calibration Verification of Instruments: Balance: ±0.1 mg; Oven temperature: 105±2°C; pH meter: ±0.02 pH units; Refractometer: ±0.0001 refractive index.
- **Key tolerance:** Balance: ±0.1 mg; Oven temperature: 105±2°C; pH meter: ±0.02 pH units; Refractometer: ±0.0001 refractive index.
- **Standards:** `ISO 126:2005`, `ISO 124:2014`, `ASTM D1076-21 Standard Specification for Rubber-Concentrated, Am`, `AST 1-2019`
